## Final-candidate execution cycle

The first frozen product candidate is `d10264be09b8a01ea643afdb39de474ce2551215`, tree `c79c34b81b7c299d5f3db2eb0afe2091c01b7a8e`. The Opus ledger is the starting inventory. No carry-over is inferred. [Opus ledger](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/4b7a359bc0994b45a91daff25065584d47ce64d8/handoff/opus-dg17-plan-20261122/ACCEPTANCE_LEDGER.md) · [Root census](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/3cafa9240d9dc557fcb50dbbfdd55bd1ac28bfe9/handoff/astra-dg16-dg18/CONTINUATION.json)

The branch applied the repo-only checker patch and corrected the known multi-failure and return-identity false accepts. It landed Stop policy v3, one shared trial deadline, attributed trial results and a typed failed-first-activation fallback. A never-bound or uncertain attempt cannot supply that fallback. Windows selected-handler controls and POSIX binding storage controls retain their respective scopes. The tests exposed baseline refusals and false accepts before correction. On the changed source the two checker suites passed 32 checks, the selected-handler suite passed 11, and the binding suite passed 28. The exact-source canonical and standalone packages verified at 588,311 and 583,706 bytes. The canonical archive has 273 bytes of headroom beyond its required reserve. Fitting the hardened version required a larger reference cut than the adviser estimated. Both clean-source staged-copy plugin selectors verified their 75-member inventory. No actual host activation was part of that check. [Exact candidate](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/commit/d10264be09b8a01ea643afdb39de474ce2551215) · [Hosted Op S](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/actions/runs/45255905134)

The first hosted batch on this candidate ended 15/16. The retained native-work-graph suite summary reports two failures and one error, with detailed stderr omitted by that runner. The successor keeps the failure and adds its diagnostic to the next in-scope run. A named custody-drift check also exposed an overly broad activation fallback on the first candidate. The successor confines fallback to value-validation refusal after rechecking admitted custody. A suite result earns original credit only if its witness reaches the named original predicate. The independent Root award process is separate. H08 needs the same L5 occurrence identified below. The combined [Op M and Op N request draft](OP_M_N_GRANT_REQUEST.md) remains unissued pending exact model provenance and a product host route that joins binding and record custody.

The custody correction candidate is `76d474064692fb4b61de42e5a11674ebc3816c6d`, tree `cc08f5caf0f878adc4a0bc34c15b702d4586d091`. The original draft treated a refusal from an intermediate custody check as a failed activation. The named test reproduced that false fallback on the first candidate and passed after rechecking custody outside value validation. Both package artifacts built with the same reserves, at 588,368 canonical and 583,767 standalone bytes. The successor reruns Op S with fuller failures and the existing topology/routing and isolated package suites. [Successor hosted run](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/actions/runs/45257482283)

The composed DG16–DG18 product is frozen at `345b0be7a7f9314051b0dd6bf65572c58be0e27a`, tree `f296fef37cf20bb9d43d83b43c61bab383422ce1`. The selected DG16 interruption correction is included. The active v4 policy allows 587,824 bytes in each archive after its required reserve. Both built inventories were verified on the clean frozen source. The [exact package and inventory record](M5_JOINT_FREEZE.json) is the input to DG16's four-route M5 qualification. The v5 approval remains historical.

Run `45257482283` retained 16/18 suite checks at the earlier custody candidate. The work-graph suite found `/usr/bin/bwrap` missing and the install suite encountered untracked Python bytecode in the package tree. The hosting workflow now provides the existing bounded ephemeral runner prerequisite and suppresses Python bytecode. It explicitly checks out the frozen product commit and verifies its tree. [Prior failed run](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/actions/runs/45257482283) · [Fixed-host Op S](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/actions/runs/45259160705)

The v4 successor for M5 is `bb781f62f1e61542ad733e1b1b652d0831e2af24`, tree `fb3b9e0acd8185651abfeb305587315d05c1e670`. It corrects the active v4 budget oracles, pins the actual builder in the active semantic fixture and restores Stop helper reachability. The earlier 345b structural run with corrected host preparation passed and retains its exact scope. The new candidate's package contract, helper owner, semantic-preservation and bootstrap controls passed locally. The fresh hosted run passed the release oracles and all 18 Op S checks. The exact product archive hashes match the freeze. The current task exercised the final closure owner against the open cold-review obligation. See the [original T3, T11 and H08 submission](final-candidate/README.md). ## T10 research copy retirement

The reviewer identified four research working copies. The two checker blobs at 02a4 matched the maintained scripts exactly. The two tests differed from their maintained counterparts only in the line selecting their adjacent checker. The existing Op S runner invokes the maintained files in `tests/`. The historical result is the only remaining reference to the duplicate directory, as the literal command it recorded. [Historical inputs and result](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/tree/48c1489a0a6544e89805de1f15c5a5f8b306d435/handoff/sol-dg17-20261122)

## N07 canonical pre-dispatch refusal baseline

The maintained governor calls `validate-run-root.sh <root>` before dispatch and treats an invalid run root as Andon. On exact f43dddd0 / `0b39f6041f1a64072c6485903220b328bdfc73c6`, the actual pre-dispatch validator accepted a visible open Andon and a phase marked `READY_TO_DISPATCH`. This is a false accept at the shipped structural gate. No dispatch, native event or acceptance is inferred. [Governor route](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/f43dddd078ed5c4c551d9e357ec90b9ac6056dc5/skills/implementaudit/SKILL.md#L272)

Tested with headless Linux Git-mode bytes. The input is the existing tracked `fixtures/run-root-example`, copied into private scratch under the exact checkout. The three altered arms substitute the current occurrence-table header before inserting one row before the ledger. The row is `| 1 | o1 | 1 | defect | Smoke B failed | restore owner output | evidence/smoke-b.log | open (rerun pending) |`. The blocked comparison changes the current phase status to `BLOCKED`. The resolved comparison changes the outcome to `resolved`. The actual owner ran normally on each root.

| Arm | STATE SHA-256 | Validator result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Existing ready exemplar | `777ad739d7da3ae9f50e76f67d65fac880a5940b7064abf39a048978a33704e7` | Exit 0 |
| Logged, open, ready | `f2befc53b9bfa60f2b060e3d760ad9173314c8df38a3f82fb89325a5f6345e74` | Exit 0, `validate-run-root: ok`. Expected refusal before dispatch. |
| Logged, open, blocked | `23d14bec50da31facb5552685c73fa8592a769e3726a8dee875f0ce380116e3a` | Exit 0 |
| Logged, resolved, ready | `6fe85b91d53d891281d3228f222966a66d1d113a225edd723209ade652418f19` | Exit 0 |

The initial command completed the unaltered positive before its display step failed. The follow-up retained the positive streams and ran only the three remaining inputs. All four used the same 123,126-byte validator, SHA-256 `7787569b4bceb541c33ee3287f0a8297882caff5478687e84fc534077e4c28ad`. The repo-only fixture checker currently distinguishes the stopped-but-invisible arm on supplied events. Canonical evidence for that arm still needs the actual failure producer and signal consumer, with an exact occurrence identity. A generic blocked state can have other causes and cannot establish that an Andon was omitted.

The source guard needs a shared join before editing. DG18's current claim initializer checks the validator's exact bytes. DG16's continuity and operational-evidence readers bind the validator in both active and frozen core profiles. The shipped Stop verifier also consumes an exact validator pin. Preserve historical tuples, compatibility and accepted subjects. The affected machine is the governor's path from current proof failure through visible Andon and blocked readiness to verified return. Occurrence/outcome, phase readiness and observed command failure are independent. The validator owns the structural refusal. The governor owns actual dispatch and the signal. A shared prospective change must keep clean/resolved roots valid, refuse affected ready roots before effects, and leave blocked recovery reachable.

## M08 removal of the ordinary no-lift path

On frozen `b30ef328c9a22f68ab420c84dc87e849fd4e4599` / tree `d79487990b4fcad5fac823c76c48406822732f13`, the normal lesson-lift owner consumed the actual phase 4 disposition. The initial focused invocation omitted the Python body and failed with SyntaxError before inspecting the artifact. The corrected invocation under fresh G0006 checked the same artifact and returned 0 before phase admission. This isolated invocation mistake had no evidence of recurrence, a broken governing gate or an unresolved obligation. The ordinary governor selected a short no-lift disposition, with no persistence or prevention claim. [Actual failure and recovery](#t6-frozen-v18-governing-reconfirmation)

The independent expectation was that the observed one-off qualifies for no-lift without a canonical lift record, a qualifying lesson without its record fails and a reasoned canonical no-lift record still passes. Ran the maintained checker on the actual proposal and an isolated one-predicate mutant on the identical proposal. The mutation dropped only the qualifying-trigger conjunct from the missing-record decision, which forced a canonical record on the one-off path.

| Input and owner | Expected | Observed |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Actual phase 4 disposition, normal owner | Accept the no-lift route | Exit 0, `check-lesson-lift: ok`, empty stderr |
| Same exact disposition, qualification condition removed | Oracle detects mandatory-record regression | Exit 1, `qualifying lesson closed without a Lesson-lift routing record` |
| Existing qualifying lesson with no record, normal owner | Refuse | Exit 1, same missing-record diagnostic |
| Existing reasoned canonical no-lift record, normal and mutated owners | Accept explicit record in both arms | Both exit 0 |

Normal owner SHA-256 `d73a873b63fe4efc7a5dadcc7b45043b68242c0afedbae6b5eb6cdf6a2630e9a`, mutant `562e65e41c9518bfdec8ebb97858131e910ae098ecaceb6c0014df34c95e7590` and shared proposal `f19ef52d22068b3961c71b1833038dac6641c2d97d59c2a1d4fc4242438b51c1`. Full governing file hash inventory matched before and after, SHA-256 `61b386f9f9fe20dd5652cf10aea96d6e60524cbea39cd6c6a6cc674abdc6449c`, and G0007 stayed current. Both checker arms stopped at their read-only decision. The frozen product remained clean. This requests M08 receiving at the ordinary lesson-lift decision scope. The warranted-learning, later prevention and whole T7 claims remain separate.

## M14 exact b30 pre-dispatch cheap-path check

The original M14 award covers observed model-policy and file effects on `a297957b`. The retained actor comparison had real unnecessary queue writes only under the policy mutant. Those actors were not replayed. The changed b30 compiler was checked at the actual maintained recursive transition function, which consumes the frontier mode, on three existing constructed selector case shapes. The case had two eligible cells but a one-seat operator ceiling, so the unchanged oracle expected one selected cell and no graph activation. The isolated comparison changed only the free-capacity threshold from two to one. [Original receiving](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/c51e40200d5e4640268c8d46fe119e2f98a96dad/handoff/pro-executor-results-20261115/continuing-execution-20261118-08/ROOT_M14_RECOVERED_ADJUDICATION_G0001.json)

| Eligible case | Normal transition | Single-guard mutant |
| --- | --- | --- |
| One available seat | `CHEAP_SERIAL`, one selected, graph not required | `GRAPH_CANDIDATE` with the same single selection, against the fixed oracle |
| Two available seats | `GRAPH_CANDIDATE`, two selected | Entire sealed transition unchanged |
| Zero available seats | `CHEAP_SERIAL`, none selected | Entire sealed transition unchanged |

Both one-seat arms consumed the same graph `a5c6213f194be421941b4700e80a64b924c2f5d60d9a0b15b24132c0728956cc`. Only the mode, graph-required flag and its derived transition digest differed. All results had authority `NONE`. Execution stopped before dispatch. The frozen compiler SHA-256 is `10a1f3f0275774b3eb71cc6a86bc32f29046716af2e4e4a0db1b099564b26ff3`, the isolated body `18b3ecb3d49c6423c0487246ce9e6fb30aa6c1bef397d2a9696ff114fc8f44ea` and the observations `c6b5cb5271bc1e8475c8109613dd1e92f1207e165e63a84cd7b9560321fa2366`. Both worktrees remained clean. This adds exact-candidate pre-dispatch discrimination to the earlier model/file-effect scope. Actual loaded child, native scheduling and whole-family award are outside this event.

## Successor-only T14 cold finding in 5S added-lines

The independent V19 cold review found that the frozen census dropped the valid source line `++count;` while retaining `count += 1;`. Its prefix filter treated any diff line beginning with three plus signs as a file header. The existing named suite, updated with those two valid additions and a multi-file header refusal, reproduced the missing line against exact V19 helper SHA-256 `98b44f2e2785c729ca24f76f9f5b3abce84873ea5228c8cb193be77ca0e0d2c3`. RED stderr SHA-256 `20c4a81d3aff18ea33c7ac3003901364f3dd9f3da2f6a1fcfd313572f4932d82`.

The successor-only helper reads addition lines only inside an actual unified-diff hunk and resets at the next file boundary. The full existing repo-state suite passed, preserving both additions, excluding Git's later file header and maintaining earlier masked-index/commit, run-root, and unrelated-path behavior. The new owner SHA-256 is `7836a71148c15c182389010cba3e81b7d7fe7f8f8e252fc3c5c676e747392659`. GREEN stdout SHA-256 `77496276f0f21b2d2d1b67dca4d8b2adb0508933fce2d834dc98798ba6f91b3e`, stderr empty.

This repair is queued in the same later successor line as any further exact cold-review correction. V19 stays immutable with its observed defect. The previous original scope decisions remain with Root. No new package freeze, registry, hosted run or protected operation was started.

## Batched successor census acquisition and line-boundary repair

Root's second cold review on `858906e7` found three further defects in the ordinary evidence producer. Forced Git color erased the additions without signalling failure. Git exit 128 could become successful empty output in both census commands. Two untracked text files with different final-newline states could be emitted as a joined line that existed in neither file. [Independent reproductions](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/commit/d3c99b0d0c28673b90b60db6d4d3cb57422c14b)

The named suite retained opposed complete-empty and failed-acquisition cases and observed the old forced-color and no-newline cases. It preserves the missing-color RED stderr `576cc70b7cffffa3ae84834d27d8883feb97f25f8a22cbaa6f1358fe26479532` and boundary RED stderr `f899b14c970cb38759e5ac727319c20dbdd3aeb9584c987d4492b5ed67b55ad1`.

Both commands now fail when their baseline or Git acquisition fails. `changed-files` completes the Git reads before publishing its path census. `added-lines` explicitly selects an uncolored internal unified diff with fixed indicators, accumulates the complete evidence in a private temporary directory, and publishes only after all reads pass. Each untracked text file supplies its own line boundaries. The earlier hunk-aware filter, staged/commit visibility and exclusions remain.

The full maintained repo-state suite passed under both WSL and Windows Git Bash, including forced-color equality, exact Git exit 128 propagation with no stdout, successful empty output and separate untracked text records. Both stdout files hash to `77496276f0f21b2d2d1b67dca4d8b2adb0508933fce2d834dc98798ba6f91b3e`, with empty stderr. New maintained helper SHA-256 `309e4483864112bd426914443402adf07e7deabc53225b1db1e86fb31eeec2d6`.

Root reconfirmed original T5 and T10 on frozen V19, preserving the demonstrated scopes and the separate cold defects. No registered work or freeze was replayed for this successor repair. **State-machine finding for the next authorized registry update — DG17-CHILD-RETURN-READMISSION.** The SKILL governor owns admission, current-state re-derivation and any subsequent route or transition. The child owns the bounded return. The validator owns only supplied-value comparison. Independent dimensions are original admission, actual return delivery, fresh governor facts, packet equality and downstream transition eligibility. Normal order is admitted child → actual return to governor → fresh re-derivation → envelope comparison → governor's separately guarded next decision. Drift discards the return for the proposed transition and sends control back to the governor. A validator VALID result confers no authority and establishes neither actual return delivery nor fresh derivation. The current observed mutation establishes the supplied-value guard and its no-effect boundary. The authentic M10 discriminator at the governing transition remains open. No new hosted, model, native or protected action occurred.
